October 5, 2026 at 4:55 pm
Re: Re: Behold the Open Web.
No matter by what mechanism the deed is done, it is reprehensible that the system here freely allows an anonymous troll to spam harmful, factually false statements about me, which are also entirely off-topic—yet it is my replies that are suppressed. I request that a moderator override the flags. Meanwhile, I can only hope that curious readers (who have JavaScript enabled) will wonder what’s hidden in the pile-up of flagged comments.
Much as I can, I must look on the bright side: This has motivated me to spend the whole day arranging and refining means to POSSE my comments. Indieweb’s POSSE concept is designed for interacting with corporate social media silos; it stands for “Publish (on your) Own Site, Syndicate Elsewhere”.1 I’ve been intending this for awhile.
The above two flags hid the following three comments by me, which can be read on the Open Web at Dirt Under The Rug:

    2026-10-03T15:13Z: “Anonymous troll is libeling me, and following me across threads to spread their personal-grudge flamewar.”
    2026-10-04T20:53Z (short; suppressed as downthread of the above): “I absolutely do not use an LLM in any way whatsoever for my writing.”
    2026-10-05T18:29Z: “When censorship is a personal attack.”

It’ll take me a long time to backfill all of my hundreds of comments on my own site. I’ve written all of my comments in a text editor outside the browser, so I do have all the Markdown sources—mostly jumbled in piles of scratch files. Going forward, in accord with POSSE principles, I’m publishing comments on my site just about simultaneously with posting here.
That includes this comment.

    `https://indieweb.org/POSSE` (Unlinkified because in my experience, Techdirt’s moderation robot hates comments with more than three hyperlinks.) ↩

						Re: Re: Anonymous troll is libeling me, and following me across threads to spread their personal-grudge flamewar.					</h1>

				<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;line-height:1.35;">

					<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">If the “community flag” button has any legitimate usage at all, people will press it on the parent comment by Anonymous Coward.  Since they probably won’t, I have no choice but to defend myself.  The context is here: <a href="https://archive.is/cajqZ#selection-8546.0-8732.4" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">https://archive.is/cajqZ#selection-8546.0-8732.4</a></div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">This anonymous reputational attack is all the more vicious, when it is directed against a comment on an article written by a Ph.D.—<em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">i.e.</em>, someone who would be familiar with the language and concepts in my GP comment, and who may take interest in intelligent discussion.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">For the record:  <strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">I absolutely never use, and absolutely never have used an LLM for any of my writing.  My grandparent comment (my OP) was written exclusively by my human brain connected to my human fingers, typing on a keyboard into a text editor—as are all of my other writings, period.</strong></div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">Anonymous Coward, yet again, take notice hereby of my demand that you must immediately cease and desist from your false and defamatory statements, retract those statements, and issue an apology.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">Your blatant attempt to use the repetition of a Big Lie to discredit me is patently motivated by your own jealousy and insecurity.  It is your implicit acknowledgment that I’m too smart and too eloquent for you to abide my existence.  You are unable to write anything like my GP comment—and you’re probably unable to read it with full comprehension.  You need to tear me down—by deterring others from reading anything I say.  Thus to erase me from existence, you literally deny my humanity.  After all, no one in their right minds will read anything they believe is slop dished out by an LLM!</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">It is symptomatic.  Although I see some remarkably smart people here, the <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">Techdirt</em> community also has a disturbing element of anti-intellectualism.  It’s been my experience that a regular commenter urged me, in substantial effect, to dumb myself down; ostensibly for my own good, he advised me to stop using “ten dollar words”.<a href="https://archive.is/2g5zF#fn-c-0fcde49a-dumb-down" rel="nofollow ugc" title="Read footnote." style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">1</a>  But you go far beyond that:  You seek to <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">extinguish</em> me.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">And who armed you?  Your actions are only a second-order effect of <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://dirtundertherug.com/ai/vampires.html" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">LLM vampires, exoparasites that kill their hosts</a>.  Ultimately, it is the “AI” companies <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://www.techdirt.com/2026/08/20/the-eu-wanted-a-deepfake-detector-it-got-an-ai-scarlet-letter/%23comment-5585036" rel="ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">and their apologists</a> who are responsible for creating the cultural poison that you here grab as your reputational murder-weapon.</div>

						<a href="https://archive.is/2g5zF#comment-5603564" style="color:rgb(127, 128, 130);box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(127, 128, 130);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;"><time datetime="2026-10-05T11:29:19-07:00" style="box-sizing:border-box;">October 5, 2026 at 11:29 am</time></a>

					<h1 style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-size:18.4px;font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;display:block;flex-basis:100%;flex-grow:1;flex-shrink:1;margin-block-end:0px;margin-block-start:0px;margin-bottom:0px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;margin-left:0px;margin-right:0px;margin-top:0px;order:1;padding-bottom:5px;padding-left:0px;padding-right:0px;padding-top:0px;">
						Re: Re: When censorship is a personal attack.					</h1>

				</header>

				<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;line-height:1.35;">

					<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">An anonymous troll is maliciously spamming <strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">factually false, reputationally poisonous misinformation</strong> about me.  They’re following me across threads for this purpose.  Their personal attacks have hereby derailed discussion of my on-topic comment.  If <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">Techdirt</em> had anything like a functional moderation policy, their moderators would treat such troll comments no differently than they treat commercial spam.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">To protect myself, I necessarily need to reply:  To post statements of correct factual information—and to make the appropriate notices and demands to an anonymous party, whom I lack any means to contact but through a public statement.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">Instead of the troll being stopped, my self-defensive comments have been <a href="https://archive.is/cSmEO#selection-1993.0-2001.4" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">censored through an opaque system called “flagged by the community”</a>.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">**My censored comments can be seen on the Open Web—<a href="https://archive.is/YqeAM#selection-2004.0-2094.8" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">partly in archives</a>, and partly <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://dirtundertherug.com/commentary/2026/10/04/notice0.html" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">on my website (where others will be added)</a>.  These latest events have compelled me to do what I should have done long ago:  Prepare to apply Indieweb POSSE principles (`https://indieweb.org/POSSE`) to <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">all</em> of my comments; I’ve been working on that since early this morning.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;"><strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">I request that the moderator override the flag,</strong> to accord me a reply to the anonymous troll who’s been given free rein to conduct factually false, malicious, completely off-topic personal attacks against me.  If platform moderation has any purpose at all, such things are surely within its ambit.</div>
<h2 style="color:rgb(34, 34, 34);font-size:18.2px;font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:0px;margin-block-start:0px;margin-bottom:0px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;margin-left:0px;margin-right:0px;margin-top:0px;padding-bottom:5px;padding-left:0px;padding-right:0px;padding-top:0px;">Notice</h2>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;"><strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">I absolutely do not use an LLM in any way whatsoever for my writing.  I never have.</strong>  This is a fact, not a matter of opinion.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">Again, take notice hereby of my demand that you must immediately cease and desist from your false and defamatory statements, retract those statements, and issue an apology.</div>

				</div>

					<h1 style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-size:18.4px;font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;display:block;flex-basis:100%;flex-grow:1;flex-shrink:1;margin-block-end:0px;margin-block-start:0px;margin-bottom:0px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;margin-left:0px;margin-right:0px;margin-top:0px;order:1;padding-bottom:5px;padding-left:0px;padding-right:0px;padding-top:0px;">
						Re: Re: Behold the Open Web.					</h1>

				</header>

				<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;line-height:1.35;">

					<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">No matter by what mechanism the deed is done, it is <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">reprehensible</em> that the system here freely allows an anonymous troll to spam harmful, factually false statements about me, which are also entirely off-topic—yet it is my replies that are suppressed.  <strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">I request that a moderator override the flags.</strong>  Meanwhile, I can only hope that curious readers <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">(who have JavaScript enabled)</em> will wonder what’s hidden in the pile-up of flagged comments.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">Much as I can, I must look on the bright side:  This has motivated me to spend the whole day arranging and refining means to POSSE my comments.  Indieweb’s POSSE concept is designed for interacting with corporate social media silos; it stands for <strong style="font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;">“Publish (on your) Own Site, Syndicate Elsewhere”.</strong><a href="https://archive.is/2g5zF#fn-c-96fb280c-posse" rel="nofollow ugc" title="Read footnote." style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);font-weight:700;box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">1</a>  I’ve been intending this for awhile.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">The above two flags hid the following three comments by me, which can be read on the Open Web at <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">Dirt Under The Rug:</em></div>
<ul style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;list-style-type:disc;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;padding-inline-start:40px;">
<li style="box-sizing:border-box;display:list-item;text-align:left;"><span style="display:none;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;text-align:start;text-align-last:start;text-indent:0px;text-transform:none;unicode-bidi:isolate;"></span>2026-10-03T15:13Z: <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://dirtundertherug.com/commentary/2026/10/03/anonymous-troll-libel.html" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">“Anonymous troll is libeling me, and following me across threads to spread their personal-grudge flamewar.”</a></li>
<li style="box-sizing:border-box;display:list-item;text-align:left;"><span style="display:none;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;text-align:start;text-align-last:start;text-indent:0px;text-transform:none;unicode-bidi:isolate;"></span>2026-10-04T20:53Z (short; suppressed as downthread of the above): <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://dirtundertherug.com/commentary/2026/10/04/notice0.html" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">“I absolutely do not use an LLM in any way whatsoever for my writing.”</a></li>
<li style="box-sizing:border-box;display:list-item;text-align:left;"><span style="display:none;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;text-align:start;text-align-last:start;text-indent:0px;text-transform:none;unicode-bidi:isolate;"></span>2026-10-05T18:29Z: <a href="https://archive.is/o/2g5zF/https://dirtundertherug.com/commentary/2026/10/05/censorship-personal-attack.html" rel="nofollow ugc" target="_blank" style="color:rgb(26, 81, 143);box-sizing:border-box;cursor:pointer;text-decoration-color:rgb(26, 81, 143);text-decoration-line:none;text-decoration-style:solid;text-decoration-thickness:auto;">“When censorship is a personal attack.”</a></li>
</ul>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">It’ll take me a long time to backfill all of my hundreds of comments on my own site.  I’ve written all of my comments in a text editor outside the browser, so I do have all the Markdown sources—mostly jumbled in piles of scratch files.  Going forward, in accord with POSSE principles, I’m publishing comments on my site just about simultaneously with posting here.</div>
<div style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;">That includes <em style="font-style:italic;box-sizing:border-box;">this</em> comment.</div>
<ol style="box-sizing:border-box;display:block;list-style-type:decimal;margin-block-end:14px;margin-block-start:14px;margin-inline-end:0px;margin-inline-start:0px;padding-inline-start:40px;">
